DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player taps (or clicks) to drop a timed bomb that clears any obstacles inside its blast radius, carving a safe path through dense hazard fields. Timing each explosion to create just‑right openings creates a visceral, satisfying loop.

## Player
The player controls a small avatar—represented as a cursor/ship—able to move freely across the play area and drop bombs at any location. Verbs: move, drop bomb.

## Core loop
Obstacles continuously stream across the screen, forming tight corridors. The player watches their approach, moves to a safe spot, and chooses when to drop a bomb. After a short delay the bomb detonates, erasing any obstacles within its radius, instantly opening a gap. The player then rushes through the newly cleared passage before the next wave arrives. Each decision to drop a bomb feels urgent, and the moment of the explosion provides immediate visual reward.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Dense moving blocks** that travel in predictable patterns, filling the screen and forcing the player to find a gap.
- **Rotating sweepers** that spin around a pivot, carving temporary safe arcs but sweeping through the player’s position if mistimed.
- **Static spikes** that appear in clusters and cannot be moved through; they can only be removed by a bomb’s blast.
- **Rapid projectiles** that zip across the field, providing a timing challenge for bomb placement.

All hazards threaten the player on contact and must be cleared or avoided.

## Score
- Clearing any obstacle with a bomb adds points.
- Surviving a wave without taking damage grants a bonus.
- Performing a chain of bomb detonations that clear multiple obstacles in quick succession yields a combo multiplier.

## Win and lose
The player loses when an obstacle contacts the avatar, triggering a game‑over overlay. The player wins by reaching the end of a predefined stage or by surviving a set duration while clearing all hazards, at which point a celebratory “You Win!” screen appears.

## Feel
Fast‑paced, tension‑filled gameplay with bright neon bursts against a dark backdrop. A synth‑driven chiptune soundtrack underscores each explosion, while visual feedback—flashing colors and particle bursts—makes every cleared path feel rewarding.

## Why it's fun
The hook is the instant, dramatic payoff when a perfectly timed bomb clears a wall of danger, giving the player a triumphant burst of space to sprint through.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML Scaffold & Meta.json — create index.html with header, canvas, restart button, empty script block, and meta.json matching header text.
2. Game State Objects — define player, hazards, score, phase objects at top of script with initial values.
3. Animation Loop — implement startLoop with cancelAnimationFrame, loop body try/catch, calling update(dt) and render(), clearing canvas.
4. Input System — set up keys Set via keydown/keyup, map R key and restart button to same reset function.
5. Player Movement — apply dt-based physics based on key inputs, ensure controls affect player state.
6. Hazard Management — create hazard data structure, spawn logic respecting SAFE_RADIUS, update motion with dt.
7. Collision Detection — detect player-hazard collisions, transition to "gameover" state and stop gameplay.
8. Scoring — increment score on discrete events, render score on canvas top-left.
9. Rendering — clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player (high contrast), and HUD each frame.
10. Game Over Overlay — draw dimmed screen with "Game Over", final score, and restart instructions.
11. Observable Contract — expose window.__game each frame with state, score, player coordinates/visibility, and objective sentence.
12. Audio Integration — add lazy AudioContext in beep() helper, trigger sounds on hit, scoring, and death events.

    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Bomb Burst</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to drop a bomb that detonates after a short delay, erasing all obstacles within its blast radius; time the explosions to carve safe passages through dense sections.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap or click to drop bomb</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  // Game state objects
  let player = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, radius: 12, alive: true };
  let hazards = []; // array of obstacles
  let score = 0;
  let phase = "menu"; // "menu" | "playing" | "gameover"

  let keys = new Set();
  let lastTime = performance.now();
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    // Clear canvas
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Draw hazards (simple rectangles for now)
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ff5722"; // bright color for obstacles
    for (const h of hazards) {
      // assume hazard has x, y, w, h properties
      ctx.fillRect(h.x, h.y, h.w, h.h);
    }

    // Draw player
    if (player.alive) {
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff"; // high contrast cyan
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, player.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // HUD - score
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);

    // Game Over overlay
    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Let's implement minimal code:

```js
// === RESET ==============================================================
function reset() {
  // reset state
  player.x = W/2;
  player.y = H/2;
  player.alive = true;
  score = 0;
  phase = "playing";
  keys.clear();
  hazards = [];
  lastTime = performance.now();
  startLoop();
}

// === LOOP ==============================================================
function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      render();
      publishState();
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("loop error", e);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

// === UPDATE ==============================================================
function update(dt) {
  if (phase !== "playing") return;
  const speed = 200; // px per second
  if (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A")) player.x -= speed * dt;
  if (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D")) player.x += speed * dt;
  if (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W")) player.y -= speed * dt;
  if (keys.has("ArrowDown") || keys.has("s") || keys.has("S")) player.y += speed * dt;
  // clamp to canvas
  player.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, player.x));
  player.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, player.y));
}

// === INPUT ==============================================================
document.addEventListener("keydown", ev => {
  if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
  keys.add(ev.key);
});
document.addEventListener("keyup", ev => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

// === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
function beep(freq, durSec) {
  try {
    if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
    const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
    osc.type = "sine";
    osc.frequency.value = freq;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
    osc.start();
    osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
  } catch {}
}

// === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ================================================
function publishState() {
  const visible = player.x >= 0 && player.x < W && player.y >= 0 && player.y < H && player.alive;
  window.__game = {
    state: phase,
    score,
    player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible },
    objective: "Drop bombs to clear obstacles and survive."
  };
}

// === BOOT ================================================================
reset();
```

Add these after render function, but before script close.

Also need to ensure no duplicate function publishState defined already? Not currently present. So safe.

Make sure we add comments with appropriate // === SECTION === labels.

prompt (15,917 chars)
## Reference example: "asteroids"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Asteroids",
  "description": "Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.",
  "controls": [
    "Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate",
    "Up (or W) to thrust",
    "Space to fire",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "shooter", "physics"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Asteroids</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #05050c; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Asteroids</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Rotate, thrust, and shoot. Big rocks split into small ones. Get hit and you're gone.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Left/Right (or A/D) to rotate</li>
      <li>Up (or W) to thrust</li>
      <li>Space to fire</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const W = 800, H = 600;
  const SHIP_RADIUS = 12;
  const SHIP_THRUST = 280;       // px/sec^2
  const SHIP_FRICTION = 0.6;     // velocity decay per second (1 = none)
  const SHIP_TURN = Math.PI * 1.8; // rad/sec
  const BULLET_SPEED = 520;      // px/sec
  const BULLET_LIFE = 1.0;       // sec
  const FIRE_COOLDOWN = 0.20;    // sec between shots
  const ASTEROID_BIG_R = 38;
  const ASTEROID_MID_R = 22;
  const ASTEROID_LIL_R = 12;
  const ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE = [40, 110];
  const SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN = 160; // no asteroid spawns inside this disk

  const SCORE_BIG = 20, SCORE_MID = 50, SCORE_LIL = 100;

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let ship;        // { x, y, vx, vy, angle, alive }
  let bullets;     // [{ x, y, vx, vy, life }]
  let asteroids;   // [{ x, y, vx, vy, r, shape: number[] }]
  let score;
  let highScore = 0;
  let phase;       // "playing" | "gameover"
  let fireCooldown;
  let keys;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    ship = { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, vx: 0, vy: 0, angle: -Math.PI / 2, alive: true };
    bullets = [];
    asteroids = [];
    spawnWave(5);
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    fireCooldown = 0;
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  function spawnWave(n) {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) spawnAsteroid(ASTEROID_BIG_R);
  }

  function spawnAsteroid(r) {
    let x, y, tries = 0;
    do {
      x = Math.random() * W;
      y = Math.random() * H;
      tries++;
    } while (Math.hypot(x - ship.x, y - ship.y) < SAFE_RADIUS_AT_SPAWN && tries < 30);
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x, y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  function makeAsteroidShape() {
    // Per-vertex radius multipliers, 8-12 vertices for a knobbly silhouette.
    const n = 8 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) out.push(0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45);
    return out;
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) {
      const turnIn = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
                   - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      ship.angle += turnIn * SHIP_TURN * dt;

      const thrusting = keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W");
      if (thrusting) {
        ship.vx += Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
        ship.vy += Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_THRUST * dt;
      }
      // Mild friction so the ship doesn't drift forever.
      const decay = Math.pow(SHIP_FRICTION, dt);
      ship.vx *= decay;
      ship.vy *= decay;
      ship.x = wrap(ship.x + ship.vx * dt, W);
      ship.y = wrap(ship.y + ship.vy * dt, H);

      fireCooldown -= dt;
      if ((keys.has(" ") || keys.has("Space")) && fireCooldown <= 0) {
        fire();
        fireCooldown = FIRE_COOLDOWN;
      }
    }

    // Bullets.
    for (const b of bullets) {
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x = wrap(b.x + b.vx * dt, W);
      b.y = wrap(b.y + b.vy * dt, H);
    }
    bullets = bullets.filter((b) => b.life > 0);

    // Asteroids.
    for (const a of asteroids) {
      a.x = wrap(a.x + a.vx * dt, W);
      a.y = wrap(a.y + a.vy * dt, H);
    }

    // Bullet-asteroid collisions.
    for (let i = asteroids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const a = asteroids[i];
      let hit = false;
      for (let j = bullets.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        const b = bullets[j];
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y) < a.r) {
          bullets.splice(j, 1);
          hit = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (hit) splitAsteroid(i);
    }

    // Ship-asteroid collisions.
    if (ship.alive) {
      for (const a of asteroids) {
        if (Math.hypot(a.x - ship.x, a.y - ship.y) < a.r + SHIP_RADIUS * 0.6) {
          ship.alive = false;
          phase = "gameover";
          if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
          beep(110, 0.3);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    // Wave clear -> spawn next.
    if (asteroids.length === 0 && phase === "playing") {
      spawnWave(5);
    }
  }

  function fire() {
    bullets.push({
      x: ship.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      y: ship.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_RADIUS,
      vx: ship.vx + Math.cos(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      vy: ship.vy + Math.sin(ship.angle) * BULLET_SPEED,
      life: BULLET_LIFE,
    });
    beep(820, 0.04);
  }

  function splitAsteroid(idx) {
    const a = asteroids[idx];
    asteroids.splice(idx, 1);
    if (a.r === ASTEROID_BIG_R) {
      score += SCORE_BIG;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_MID_R);
      beep(440, 0.06);
    } else if (a.r === ASTEROID_MID_R) {
      score += SCORE_MID;
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) addChild(a, ASTEROID_LIL_R);
      beep(560, 0.05);
    } else {
      score += SCORE_LIL;
      beep(700, 0.05);
    }
  }

  function addChild(parent, r) {
    const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
    const speed = ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0] + Math.random() * (ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[1] - ASTEROID_SPEED_RANGE[0]);
    asteroids.push({
      x: parent.x, y: parent.y,
      vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,
      vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,
      r,
      shape: makeAsteroidShape(),
    });
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#05050c";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // Asteroids.
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#a5a5b8";
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    for (const a of asteroids) drawAsteroid(a);

    // Bullets.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffd23f";
    for (const b of bullets) {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, 2.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }

    // Ship.
    if (ship.alive) drawShip();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Best ${highScore}`, W - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "gameover") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(5,5,12,0.65)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText("Game Over", W / 2, H / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, W / 2, H / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", W / 2, H / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  function drawShip() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
    ctx.rotate(ship.angle);
    ctx.strokeStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(SHIP_RADIUS, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.9, -SHIP_RADIUS * 0.7);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
    if (keys && (keys.has("ArrowUp") || keys.has("w") || keys.has("W"))) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = "#ffd23f";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(-SHIP_RADIUS * 0.5, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(-SHIP_RADIUS - 6 - Math.random() * 4, 0);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawAsteroid(a) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i < a.shape.length; i++) {
      const t = (i / a.shape.length) * Math.PI * 2;
      const r = a.r * a.shape[i];
      const x = a.x + Math.cos(t) * r;
      const y = a.y + Math.sin(t) * r;
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
    if (ev.key === " ") ev.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase,                                  // "playing" | "gameover"
      score,
      player: {
        x: ship.x,
        y: ship.y,
        // wrap() keeps coords in [0, W) and [0, H), so visible is always true
        // — but assert it explicitly so the contract is honest.
        visible: ship.x >= 0 && ship.x < W && ship.y >= 0 && ship.y < H,
      },
      objective: "Shoot the asteroids before they hit your ship. Big rocks split into smaller ones.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function wrap(v, max) {
    if (v < 0) return v + max;
    if (v >= max) return v - max;
    return v;
  }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

```js
// === UPDATE =============================================================
// Player movement and hazard updates

const PLAYER_SPEED = 200; // px/s

function update(dt) {
  // Player movement based on keys (arrow keys or WASD)
  let dx = 0, dy = 0;
  if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) dx -= 1;
  if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) dx += 1;
  if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) dy -= 1;
  if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) dy += 1;
  if (dx !== 0 || dy !== 0) {
    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    const normX = dx / len;
    const normY = dy / len;
    player.x += normX * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
    player.y += normY * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
    // clamp within canvas
    player.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, player.x));
    player.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, player.y));
  }

  // Update hazards
  for (const h of hazards) {
    h.x += h.vx * dt;
    h.y += h.vy * dt;
  }

  // Remove hazards that are out of bounds
  hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.x >= -h.r && h.x <= W + h.r && h.y >= -h.r && h.y <= H + h.r);
}
```

```js
function spawnHazard() {
  const r = HAZARD_RADIUS;
  let x = -r;
  let y = Math.random() * H;
  const speed = HAZARD_MIN_SPEED + Math.random() * (HAZARD_MAX_SPEED - HAZARD_MIN_SPEED);
  const vx = speed;
  const vy = 0;
  // Ensure safe radius from player at spawn
  const dist = Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y);
  if (dist < SAFE_RADIUS) {
    // push further away by moving farther left
    x = -r - (SAFE_RADIUS - dist);
  }
  hazards.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r });
}

Thus edit file: identify `let audioCtx = null;` and after that, add code:

```
  // === UPDATE =============================================================

  const PLAYER_SPEED = 200; // px/sec

  const HAZARD_RADIUS = 14;
  const HAZARD_MIN_SPEED = 80;
  const HAZARD_MAX_SPEED = 150;
  const SAFE_RADIUS = 120;
  const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.5;

  let spawnTimer = 0;

  function spawnHazard() {
    const r = HAZARD_RADIUS;
    let x = -r;
    let y = Math.random() * H;
    // velocity towards right
    const speed = HAZARD_MIN_SPEED + Math.random() * (HAZARD_MAX_SPEED - HAZARD_MIN_SPEED);
    const vx = speed;
    const vy = 0;
    // Ensure safe distance from player at spawn
    if (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS) {
      // shift farther left to maintain safe distance
      x = -r - (SAFE_RADIUS - Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y));
    }
    hazards.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r });
  }

  function update(dt) {
    // Player movement
    let dirX = 0, dirY = 0;
    if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) dirX -= 1;
    if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) dirX += 1;
    if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) dirY -= 1;
    if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) dirY += 1;
    if (dirX !== 0 || dirY !== 0) {
      const len = Math.hypot(dirX, dirY);
      const nx = dirX / len;
      const ny = dirY / len;
      player.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, player.x + nx * PLAYER_SPEED * dt));
      player.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, player.y + ny * PLAYER_SPEED * dt));
    }

    // Hazard spawning
    spawnTimer -= dt;
    if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
      spawnHazard();
      spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
    }

    // Update hazards
    for (const h of hazards) {
      h.x += h.vx * dt;
      h.y += h.vy * dt;
    }
    // Remove off-screen hazards
    hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.x < W + h.r && h.x > -h.r && h.y < H + h.r && h.y > -h.r);
  }
```

// px/sec\n\n  const HAZARD_RADIUS = 14;\n  const HAZARD_MIN_SPEED = 80;\n  const HAZARD_MAX_SPEED = 150;\n  const SAFE_RADIUS = 120;\n  const SPAWN_INTERVAL = 1.5;\n\n  let spawnTimer = 0;\n\n  function spawnHazard() {\n    const r = HAZARD_RADIUS;\n    let x = -r;\n    let y = Math.random() * H;\n    // velocity towards right\n    const speed = HAZARD_MIN_SPEED + Math.random() * (HAZARD_MAX_SPEED - HAZARD_MIN_SPEED);\n    const vx = speed;\n    const vy = 0;\n    // Ensure safe distance from player at spawn\n    if (Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y) < SAFE_RADIUS) {\n      // shift farther left to maintain safe distance\n      x = -r - (SAFE_RADIUS - Math.hypot(x - player.x, y - player.y));\n    }\n    hazards.push({ x, y, vx, vy, r });\n  }\n\n  function update(dt) {\n    // Player movement\n    let dirX = 0, dirY = 0;\n    if (keys.has('ArrowLeft') || keys.has('a') || keys.has('A')) dirX -= 1;\n    if (keys.has('ArrowRight') || keys.has('d') || keys.has('D')) dirX += 1;\n    if (keys.has('ArrowUp') || keys.has('w') || keys.has('W')) dirY -= 1;\n    if (keys.has('ArrowDown') || keys.has('s') || keys.has('S')) dirY += 1;\n    if (dirX !== 0 || dirY !== 0) {\n      const len = Math.hypot(dirX, dirY);\n      const nx = dirX / len;\n      const ny = dirY / len;\n      player.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, player.x + nx * PLAYER_SPEED * dt));\n      player.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, player.y + ny * PLAYER_SPEED * dt));\n    }\n\n    // Hazard spawning\n    spawnTimer -= dt;\n    if (spawnTimer <= 0) {\n      spawnHazard();\n      spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;\n    }\n\n    // Update hazards\n    for (const h of hazards) {\n      h.x += h.vx * dt;\n      h.y += h.vy * dt;\n    }\n    // Remove off-screen hazards\n    hazards = hazards.filter(h => h.x < W + h.r && h.x > -h.r && h.y < H + h.r && h.y > -h.r);\n  }\n```\n\nHowever need to be careful: we defined hazards as 'let hazards = []' earlier; Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Bomb Burst
Concept: Tap to drop a bomb that detonates after a short delay, erasing all obstacles within its blast radius; time the explosions to carve safe passages through dense sections.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path.
